 /*********************************************************************/
0 SELECT
    'TODAYS DATA HAS ALREADY BEEN LOADED' (NAMED ERROR_MSG)
    ,AUD.RUN_CONTROL_DTE         (NAMED PERIOD_DTE)
    ,AUD.TOTAL_INSERTS
    ,AUD.TABLE_NAME
0   FROM
     AUDIT_LOG  AUD
    CROSS JOIN
     FDM_RUN_DATE DTE
0   WHERE
     AUD.TABLE_NAME = 'RSDL_BAL_CALC_PERIODIC'
    AND
     AUD.STEP = 'SQREBL40'
    AND
     AUD.RUN_CONTROL_DTE = DTE.RUN_CONTROL_DTE
    ;

0SELECT DISTINCT
  AAR.WH_ACC_NO
 ,AAR.APPL_ID
 ,SNP.APPL_PROD_ID
 ,CAST(SUBSTRING(SNP.BOOKED_TIME,1,10) AS DATE)   AS BOOKED_DATE
 ,CAST(SNP.BOOKED_TIME AS TIMESTAMP(6))           AS BOOKED_TIME1
 ,SNP.PROD_CDE
 ,SNP.EXPIRY_DTE
 ,SNP.REPAYMT_FREQ_CDE
 ,SNP.TRANS_DTE
 ,SNP.DEFLT_REPAYMT_AMT
 ,SNP.FIRST_REPAYMT_DTE
 ,SNP.REPAYMT_NO
 ,SNP.TERM
 ,ADDN.ADDN_TERM_REPAYMT_NO
 ,ADDN.ADDN_TERM_FIRST_REPAYMT_DTE
 ,CASE WHEN
        SNP.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE = 'IFC'
       THEN
        SNP.TERM-TERM_2
 END AS
  TERM_1
 ,ADDN.ADDN_TERM_PROD_MTHS_NO                    AS TERM_2
 ,ADDN.STD_PROD_INSTALMT_AMT
 ,SNP.INT_RTE
 ,SNP.INT_ADJ_RTE_SIGN_CDE
 ,SNP.INT_ADJ_RTE
 ,CASE WHEN
        SNP.INT_ADJ_RTE_SIGN_CDE = 'P'
       THEN
        SNP.INT_RTE + SNP.INT_ADJ_RTE
       ELSE
        SNP.INT_RTE - SNP.INT_ADJ_RTE
 END AS
  INT_RTE_TOTAL
 ,SNP.ACC_LIMIT_AMT*-1                           AS ACC_LIMIT_AMT1
 ,SNP.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE
 ,DER_SP.SEAS_PAY_JAN_IND
 ,DER_SP.SEAS_PAY_FEB_IND
 ,DER_SP.SEAS_PAY_MAR_IND
 ,DER_SP.SEAS_PAY_APR_IND
 ,DER_SP.SEAS_PAY_MAY_IND
 ,DER_SP.SEAS_PAY_JUN_IND
 ,DER_SP.SEAS_PAY_JUL_IND
 ,DER_SP.SEAS_PAY_AUG_IND
 ,DER_SP.SEAS_PAY_SEP_IND
 ,DER_SP.SEAS_PAY_OCT_IND
 ,DER_SP.SEAS_PAY_NOV_IND
 ,DER_SP.SEAS_PAY_DEC_IND
 ,SEAS_PAY_JAN_IND
 + SEAS_PAY_FEB_IND
 + SEAS_PAY_MAR_IND
 + SEAS_PAY_APR_IND
 + SEAS_PAY_MAY_IND
 + SEAS_PAY_JUN_IND
 + SEAS_PAY_JUL_IND
 + SEAS_PAY_AUG_IND
 + SEAS_PAY_SEP_IND
 + SEAS_PAY_OCT_IND
 + SEAS_PAY_NOV_IND
 + SEAS_PAY_DEC_IND          AS SEAS_PAY_MTH_CNT
 ,AAR.SRCE_SYS               AS APPL_SRCE_SYS
0FROM
  APPLICATION_ACCOUNT_REL            AAR
0INNER JOIN
  SUB_NAPS_PRODUCT         SNP
 ON
  SNP.APPL_ID = AAR.APPL_ID
 AND
  SNP.SRCE_SYS = AAR.SRCE_SYS
 AND
  SNP.APPL_PROD_ID = AAR.ITEM_SEQ_NO
0LEFT JOIN
  APPLICATION_NAPS_TERM_DETAIL    ADDN
 ON
  SNP.APPL_ID = ADDN.APPL_ID
 AND
  SNP.APPL_PROD_ID = ADDN.APPL_PROD_ID
0LEFT OUTER JOIN
 (
 SELECT
 NRA.APPL_ID
 ,MAX(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NRA.TRANS_DTE) = 1
           THEN
            '01'
           ELSE
            ''
      END)AS SEAS_PAY_JAN_IND
 ,MAX(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NRA.TRANS_DTE) = 2
           THEN
            '02'
           ELSE
            ''
      END)AS SEAS_PAY_FEB_IND
 ,MAX(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NRA.TRANS_DTE) = 3
           THEN
            '03'
           ELSE
            ''
      END)AS SEAS_PAY_MAR_IND
 ,MAX(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NRA.TRANS_DTE) = 4
           THEN
            '04'
           ELSE
            ''
      END)AS SEAS_PAY_APR_IND
 ,MAX(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NRA.TRANS_DTE) = 5
           THEN
            '05'
           ELSE
            ''
      END)AS SEAS_PAY_MAY_IND
 ,MAX(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NRA.TRANS_DTE) = 6
           THEN
           '06'
           ELSE
           ''
      END)AS SEAS_PAY_JUN_IND
 ,MAX(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NRA.TRANS_DTE) = 7
           THEN
            '07'
           ELSE
            ''
      END)AS SEAS_PAY_JUL_IND
 ,MAX(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NRA.TRANS_DTE) = 8
           THEN
            '08'
           ELSE
            ''
    END)AS SEAS_PAY_AUG_IND
 ,MAX(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NRA.TRANS_DTE) = 9
           THEN
           '09'
           ELSE
            ''
      END)AS SEAS_PAY_SEP_IND
 ,MAX(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NRA.TRANS_DTE) = 10
           THEN
            '10'
           ELSE
            ''
      END)AS SEAS_PAY_OCT_IND
 ,MAX(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NRA.TRANS_DTE) = 11
           THEN
            '11'
           ELSE
            ''
      END)AS SEAS_PAY_NOV_IND
 ,MAX(CASE WHEN EXTRACT(MONTH FROM NRA.TRANS_DTE) = 12
           THEN
            '12'
           ELSE
            ''
      END)AS SEAS_PAY_DEC_IND
 FROM
  NAPS_REPAYMENT_APPL_DETAIL NRA
0WHERE
  NRA.APPL_TRANS_TYP = 'X'
0GROUP BY
  NRA.APPL_ID )DER_SP
 ON
  SNP.APPL_ID = DER_SP.APPL_ID
0WHERE
    SNP.BOOKED_CDE = 'B'
  AND
    SNP.BOOKED_TIME IS NOT NULL

 ,SUB.BOOKED_TIME1
 ,SUB.EXPIRY_DTE
 ,SUB.APPL_SRCE_SYS
 ,SUB.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE
 ,CASE
      WHEN SUB.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE = 'IFC'
      THEN
       'Int Only For Fixed Period Followed by C&I'
      WHEN
       SUB.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE = 'HOL'
      THEN
       'Repayment Holiday'
      WHEN
       SUB.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE = 'SEA'
      THEN
       'Seasonal Repayments'
      WHEN
       SUB.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE = 'NON'
      THEN
       'None'
      WHEN
       SUB.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE = 'ADO'
      THEN
      'Offered repayments with Adhoc repayments'
      WHEN
       SUB.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE = 'ADS'
      THEN
      'Ad hoc repayments with calculated repayments'
      WHEN
       SUB.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE = 'INT'
      THEN
       'Final bullet with interest only repayment'
      WHEN
       SUB.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE = 'MOR'
      THEN
       'Moratorium'
      WHEN
       SUB.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE = 'ONE'
      THEN
      'Final bullet with no other repayment'
      WHEN
       SUB.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE = 'ADB'
      THEN
       'Offered repayments with final bullet'
      WHEN
      SUB.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE = 'ROB'
     THEN
      'Final bullet with offered repayment'
     WHEN
      SUB.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE IS NULL
     THEN
     'Not NAPS'
     ELSE
      SUB.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE
 END

 END

 NOT IN ('NON','SEA','HOL')
 AND REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE
 IS NOT NULL
      THEN
       1
      ELSE
       0
  END

      1
     ELSE
      0
  END AS
    NON_NAPS

     THEN

  END

   LAST_DAY(ADD_MONTHS
  (RBAB.NXT_LN_PMT_DTE - 3, (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1)*3))
  WHEN
   RBAB.REPAYMT_DAY <> NXT_LN_PMT_DAY
  AND
   RBAB.REPAYMT_INTERVAL = 3
  AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM
  (ADD_MONTHS (RBAB.NXT_LN_PMT_DTE,
  (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1)*3)))
  IN
   (4, 6, 9, 11) AND RBAB.REPAYMT_DAY = 31
  THEN
   ADD_MONTHS
  (RBAB.NXT_LN_PMT_DTE,
  (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1)*3) + REPAYMT_DAY_DIFF - 1
  WHEN
   RBAB.REPAYMT_DAY <> NXT_LN_PMT_DAY
  AND
   RBAB.REPAYMT_INTERVAL = 3
  THEN
   ADD_MONTHS (RBAB.NXT_LN_PMT_DTE,
  (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1)*3) +
    REPAYMT_DAY_DIFF
 END
 END AS
  PAYMENT_DATE
 ,COALESCE(LAG( PAYMENT_DATE) OVER
 (PARTITION BY RBAB.WH_ACC_NO,RBAB.APPL_ID, RBAB.APPL_PROD_ID
  ORDER BY
   PAYMENT_DATE DESC),
  CAST((RBAB.NXT_REPRICING_DTE +10)
  AS DATE)) AS NEXT_PAYMENT_DATE
 ,RBAB.PERIOD_DTE

       THEN
        1
       ELSE

 END AS
  FINAL_PAYMENT_IND
 ,CASE WHEN
        NAPS_REPAYMT_NO > PAYMENT_COUNTER
       THEN
        0
       WHEN
        NAPS_REPAYMT_NO = PAYMENT_COUNTER
       AND
        PAYMENT_FLAG = 1
       THEN
        1
       WHEN
        NAPS_REPAYMT_NO = PAYMENT_COUNTER
       AND
        PAYMENT_FLAG <> 1
      THEN
       2
      ELSE
       2
 END AS
  CLOSED_IND

 INSERT INTO RSDL_BAL_CALC_PERIODIC
 (
  WH_ACC_NO
 ,ACC_NO
 ,APPL_ID
 ,APPL_PROD_ID
 ,UNIQUE_KEY_INT
 ,MIN_REPAYMENT
 ,TERM_NO_UNITS
 ,REPAYMT_AMT
 ,REPAYMT_UNIT
 ,REPAYMT_INTERVAL
 ,REPAYMT_DAY
 ,ACCRD_INT
 ,CURRENT_CNTRACT_MATURITY_DTE
 ,CNTRACT_MATURITY_DTE
 ,NXT_REPRICING_DTE
 ,PROD_CDE
 ,REPAYMT_EXCPTN_DESCR
 ,EXPIRY_DTE
 ,EXPIRY_NXT_WORKDAY_DTE
 ,POSTED_DTE
 ,DATE_ROW_NUMBER
 ,NAPS_REPAYMT_NO
 ,INT_RTE
 ,INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG
 ,INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG
 ,NO_DAYS
 ,PAYMENT_COUNTER
 ,PAYMENT_FLAG
 ,REPAYMT_AMT_FINAL
 ,DR_INT_CAT
 ,NET_BAL_FOR_PRNCPL
 ,RESIDUAL_BAL_AMT
 ,RESIDUAL_BAL_IND
 ,EXCLUDED_IND
 ,DIFF_EXPIRY_DATES
 ,WEEKLY_REPAYMTS
 ,REPAYMT_AMT_0
 ,REPAYMT_INTERVAL_0
 ,OTHER_REPAYMT_TYPE
 ,NON_NAPS
 ,EXPIRED
 ,REASON
 ,PERIOD_DTE
 ,SRCE_SYS
 ,SRCE_INST
 ,LOAD_DTE
 ,LOAD_TIME
 )
0SELECT
   BASE.WH_ACC_NO
  ,BASE.ACC_NO
  ,BASE.APPL_ID
  ,BASE.APPL_PROD_ID
  ,BASE.UNIQUE_KEY_INT
  ,CASE WHEN EXCLUDED_IND = 'Y'
        THEN
         NULL
        ELSE
         BASE.MIN_REPAYMENT
   END AS MIN_REPAYMENT
0 ,BASE.TERM_NO_UNITS
  ,BASE.REPAYMT_AMT
  ,BASE.REPAYMT_UNIT
  ,BASE.REPAYMT_INTERVAL
  ,BASE.REPAYMT_DAY

  ,BASE.CURRENT_CNTRACT_MATURITY_DTE
  ,BASE.CNTRACT_MATURITY_DTE

  ,BASE.EXPIRY_DTE
  ,BASE.EXPIRY_NXT_WORKDAY_DTE
  ,RB.POSTED_DTE
  ,RB.DATE_ROW_NUMBER
  ,BASE.NAPS_REPAYMT_NO
  ,BASE.INT_RTE
  ,RB.INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG
  ,RB.INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG

  ,BASE.DR_INT_CAT
  ,BASE.NET_BAL_FOR_PRNCPL
  ,CASE WHEN
    RB.INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG = 1
   AND
   RB.PAYMENT_FLAG = 1
   AND
   RB.BALANCE < 0
        THEN
         CAST(RB.BALANCE AS DECIMAL(18,2))
        WHEN
         RB.INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG = 1
        AND
         RB.PAYMENT_FLAG = 1
        AND
         RB.BALANCE >= 0
        THEN
        CAST(0 AS DECIMAL(18,2))
       WHEN
        RB.INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG = 1
       AND
        RB.POSTED_DTE = RB.EXPIRY_NXT_WORKDAY_DTE
       AND
        RB.BALANCE < 0
       THEN
        CAST(RB.BALANCE AS DECIMAL(18,2))
       WHEN
        RB.INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG = 1
       AND
        RB.POSTED_DTE = RB.EXPIRY_NXT_WORKDAY_DTE
       AND
        RB.BALANCE >= 0
       THEN
        CAST(0 AS DECIMAL(18,2))
       WHEN
        RB.BALANCE + TRUNC(RB.FORECAST_INTEREST_CUMULATIVE) < 0
       THEN
        CAST(RB.BALANCE + TRUNC(RB.FORECAST_INTEREST_CUMULATIVE,2)
        AS DECIMAL(18,2))
       WHEN
        RB.BALANCE + TRUNC(RB.FORECAST_INTEREST_CUMULATIVE) > 0
       THEN
        0
       ELSE
        0
 END AS
  RESIDUAL_BAL_AMT
0,CASE WHEN RESIDUAL_BAL_AMT < 0
       THEN
        1
       ELSE
        0
 END AS
  RESIDUAL_BAL_IND

 ,DIFF_EXPIRY_DATES
 ,WEEKLY_REPAYMTS

  REPAYMT_INTERVAL_0 + OTHER_REPAYMT_TYPE + NON_NAPS + EXPIRED >1
       THEN
        'MULTIPLE'
       WHEN
      BASE.DIFF_EXPIRY_DATES = 1
     THEN
      COALESCE('EXPIRY DATES DIFFERENT','')
     WHEN
      BASE.WEEKLY_REPAYMTS = 1
     THEN
      COALESCE('WEEKLY REPAYMENTS','')
     WHEN
      BASE.REPAYMT_AMT_0 = 1
     THEN
      COALESCE ('REPAYMENT AMT IS 0', '')
     WHEN

     THEN

     WHEN
      BASE.OTHER_REPAYMT_TYPE = 1
     THEN
      COALESCE ('BULLET OR OTHER REPAYMT SCHED', '')
     WHEN
     BASE.NON_NAPS = 1
    THEN
     COALESCE ('NO NAPS APPLICATION', '')
    WHEN
     BASE.EXPIRED = 1
    THEN
     COALESCE ('EXPIRED ON REPORT DATE', '')
    WHEN
     EXCLUDED_IND = 'N'
    THEN
    'RESIDUAL BAL CALCD'
    ELSE
     NULL
 END AS
  REASON
 ,BASE.PERIOD_DTE
 ,BASE.APPL_SRCE_SYS AS SRCE_SYS

0  INSERT INTO AUDIT_LOG
  (
   TABLE_NAME
  ,STEP
  ,SRCE_SYS
  ,SRCE_INST
  ,TOTAL_INSERTS
  ,TOTAL_UPDATES
  ,TOTAL_DELETES
  ,RUN_CONTROL_DTE
  ,LOAD_DTE
  ,LOAD_TIME
  ,LOG_DTE
  ,LOG_TIME
  )
0  SELECT
   'RSDL_BAL_CALC_PERIODIC'
  ,'SQREBL40'
  ,COALESCE(REB.SRCE_SYS, 0)
  ,REB.SRCE_INST
  ,COUNT(*)
  ,0
  ,0
  ,DTE.RUN_CONTROL_DTE
  ,DTE.LOAD_DTE
  ,DTE.LOAD_TIME
  ,DATE
  ,TIME
0  FROM
    RSDL_BAL_CALC_PERIODIC REB
0  CROSS JOIN
    FDM_RUN_DATE DTE
0  GROUP BY
     DTE.RUN_CONTROL_DTE
    ,REB.SRCE_SYS
    ,REB.SRCE_INST
    ,DTE.LOAD_DTE
    ,DTE.LOAD_TIME
    ;

0SELECT DISTINCT
  AAR.WH_ACC_NO
 ,AAR.APPL_ID
 ,SNP.APPL_PROD_ID
 ,CAST(SUBSTRING(SNP.BOOKED_TIME,1,10) AS DATE)   AS BOOKED_DATE
 ,CAST(SNP.BOOKED_TIME AS TIMESTAMP(6))           AS BOOKED_TIME1
 ,SNP.PROD_CDE
 ,SNP.EXPIRY_DTE
 ,SNP.REPAYMT_FREQ_CDE
 ,SNP.TRANS_DTE
 ,SNP.DEFLT_REPAYMT_AMT
 ,SNP.FIRST_REPAYMT_DTE
 ,SNP.REPAYMT_NO
 ,SNP.TERM
 ,ADDN.ADDN_TERM_REPAYMT_NO
 ,ADDN.ADDN_TERM_FIRST_REPAYMT_DTE
 ,CASE WHEN
        SNP.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE = 'IFC'
       THEN
        SNP.TERM-TERM_2
 END AS
  TERM_1
 ,ADDN.ADDN_TERM_PROD_MTHS_NO                    AS TERM_2
 ,ADDN.STD_PROD_INSTALMT_AMT
 ,SNP.INT_RTE
 ,SNP.INT_ADJ_RTE_SIGN_CDE
 ,SNP.INT_ADJ_RTE
 ,CASE WHEN
        SNP.INT_ADJ_RTE_SIGN_CDE = 'P'
       THEN
        SNP.INT_RTE + SNP.INT_ADJ_RTE
       ELSE
        SNP.INT_RTE - SNP.INT_ADJ_RTE
 END AS
  INT_RTE_TOTAL
 ,SNP.ACC_LIMIT_AMT*-1                           AS ACC_LIMIT_AMT1
 ,SNP.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_CDE

0LEFT JOIN

0LEFT OUTER JOIN

            '01'

 /* UPDATE  SQRSBL10_RSDL_BAL_ACC_BASE                               */

 UPDATE SQRSBL10_RSDL_BAL_ACC_BASE
 FROM (
 SELECT DISTINCT RBAB.EXPIRY_DTE
          ,MIN(AMC.POSTED_DTE)
 OVER(PARTITION BY RBAB.EXPIRY_DTE)
 AS NXT_WKG_DAY
0FROM
  SQRSBL10_RSDL_BAL_ACC_BASE RBAB
 INNER JOIN
  RESI_BAL_CAL_REF        AMC
 ON
  AMC.APR_REGION = 'ROI'
 AND
  AMC. APR_REGION_HOLIDAY_IND = 'WD'
 AND
  AMC.POSTED_DTE
 BETWEEN
  RBAB.EXPIRY_DTE AND RBAB.EXPIRY_DTE + 6)
  TMP (EXP_DTE, NXT_WKG_DAY)
 SET
  EXPIRY_NXT_WORKDAY_DTE = TMP.NXT_WKG_DAY
 WHERE
  EXPIRY_DTE = TMP.EXP_DTE;

0SELECT DISTINCT

  ,RBAB.APPL_ID

0 ,CASE WHEN NL.NO_REPAYMENTS = 1
        THEN
         RBAB.NXT_LN_PMT_DTE ELSE
   CASE
   WHEN
    RBAB.REPAYMT_DAY = NXT_LN_PMT_DAY
   AND
    RBAB.REPAYMT_INTERVAL = 1
        THEN ADD_MONTHS
        (RBAB.NXT_LN_PMT_DTE, (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1))
        WHEN
         RBAB.REPAYMT_DAY = NXT_LN_PMT_DAY
        AND
         RBAB.REPAYMT_INTERVAL = 7
        THEN

        AND
         RBAB.REPAYMT_INTERVAL = 14
        THEN

        AND

        WHEN

        AND
         RBAB.REPAYMT_INTERVAL = 6
        THEN ADD_MONTHS
        (RBAB.NXT_LN_PMT_DTE, (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1)*6)
        WHEN
         RBAB.REPAYMT_DAY = NXT_LN_PMT_DAY
        AND
         RBAB.REPAYMT_INTERVAL = 3
        THEN ADD_MONTHS
        (RBAB.NXT_LN_PMT_DTE, (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1)*3)
        WHEN
         RBAB.REPAYMT_DAY <> NXT_LN_PMT_DAY
        AND
         RBAB.REPAYMT_INTERVAL = 7
        THEN
         RBAB.NXT_LN_PMT_DTE  + (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1)*7
        WHEN
          RBAB.REPAYMT_DAY <> NXT_LN_PMT_DAY
         AND
          RBAB.REPAYMT_INTERVAL = 14
         THEN
          RBAB.NXT_LN_PMT_DTE  + (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1)*14
         WHEN
          RBAB.REPAYMT_DAY <> NXT_LN_PMT_DAY
         AND
          RBAB.REPAYMT_INTERVAL = 1 AND EXTRACT(MONTH
 FROM
  (ADD_MONTHS (RBAB.NXT_LN_PMT_DTE,
  (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1))))
  IN (2) AND NXT_LN_PMT_DAY
  = 28
  THEN
   LAST_DAY(ADD_MONTHS (RBAB.NXT_LN_PMT_DTE,
  (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1)))
  WHEN
   RBAB.REPAYMT_DAY <> NXT_LN_PMT_DAY
 AND
  RBAB.REPAYMT_INTERVAL = 1 AND EXTRACT

  (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1))))
  IN (2) AND NXT_LN_PMT_DAY
  = 29
  THEN
   LAST_DAY(ADD_MONTHS (RBAB.NXT_LN_PMT_DTE
  - 1, (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1)))
  WHEN
   RBAB.REPAYMT_DAY <> NXT_LN_PMT_DAY
  AND
   RBAB.REPAYMT_INTERVAL = 1 AND EXTRACT(MONTH
 FROM
  (ADD_MONTHS (RBAB.NXT_LN_PMT_DTE,
  (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1))))
  IN (2) AND NXT_LN_PMT_DAY
  = 30
  THEN
   LAST_DAY(ADD_MONTHS (RBAB.NXT_LN_PMT_DTE
  - 2, (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1)))
  WHEN
   RBAB.REPAYMT_DAY <> NXT_LN_PMT_DAY
  AND
   RBAB.REPAYMT_INTERVAL = 1 AND EXTRACT
  (MONTH FROM (ADD_MONTHS (RBAB.NXT_LN_PMT_DTE,
  (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1))))
  IN
   (2) AND NXT_LN_PMT_DAY
  = 31
  THEN
   LAST_DAY(ADD_MONTHS (RBAB.NXT_LN_PMT_DTE
  - 3, (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1)))
  WHEN
   RBAB.REPAYMT_DAY <> NXT_LN_PMT_DAY AND
   RBAB.REPAYMT_INTERVAL = 1 AND EXTRACT(MONTH FROM
  (ADD_MONTHS (RBAB.NXT_LN_PMT_DTE,
  (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1))))   IN (4, 6, 9, 11)

   RBAB.REPAYMT_DAY = 31

   RBAB.REPAYMT_DAY <> NXT_LN_PMT_DAY

   RBAB.REPAYMT_INTERVAL = 1

  (NL.NO_REPAYMENTS-1)) + REPAYMT_DAY_DIFF

   RBAB.REPAYMT_INTERVAL = 12

  BASE.POSTED_DTE >= PRPD.PAYMENT_DATE
 AND
  BASE.POSTED_DTE < PRPD.NEXT_PAYMENT_DATE
     THEN
      PRPD.NO_REPAYMENTS - 1
     WHEN
      BASE.POSTED_DTE = BASE.PERIOD_DTE
     THEN
      0
     WHEN
      BASE.POSTED_DTE = BASE.MIN_REPAYMENT
     THEN
      1
     ELSE
      NULL
     END
     ELSE

0SELECT
     SUBT1.UNIQUE_KEY_INT
    ,SUBT1.WH_ACC_NO
    ,SUBT1.POSTED_DTE
    ,BASE.ACCRD_INT  * -1 AS ACCRUED_INTEREST_FORECAST
    ,SUBT1.EXPIRY_NXT_WORKDAY_DTE
    ,SUBT1.MIN_REPAYMENT
    ,SUBT1.INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG
    ,SUBT1.INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG
    ,SUBT1.FW_TEST
    ,SUBT1.PAYMENT_COUNTER
    ,SUBT1.FORECAST_INTEREST_DAILY
    ,SUBT1.FORECAST_INTEREST_CUMULATIVE
    ,SUBT1.FORECAST_TOTAL_INTEREST
    ,SUBT1.FORECAST_TOTAL_REPAYMENTS
    ,SUBT1.PERIOD_DTE
    ,ROW_NUMBER() OVER
 (PARTITION BY SUBT1.UNIQUE_KEY_INT
 ORDER BY
  SUBT1.POSTED_DTE)
 AS TRANS_ORDER
  ,LAG(PAYMENT_COUNTER) OVER
 (PARTITION BY SUBT1.UNIQUE_KEY_INT
 ORDER BY
  SUBT1.POSTED_DTE)
 AS LAGGED_PAYMENT_COUNTER
0,CASE WHEN
        TRANS_ORDER = 1 AND POSTED_DTE = SUBT1.MIN_REPAYMENT
       THEN
     1
       WHEN
        TRANS_ORDER = 1
       AND
        POSTED_DTE <> SUBT1.MIN_REPAYMENT
       THEN
        0
       WHEN
         LAGGED_PAYMENT_COUNTER IS NULL
       AND
        PAYMENT_COUNTER = 1
       THEN
        1
 ELSE
  PAYMENT_COUNTER - LAGGED_PAYMENT_COUNTER END
 AS PAYMENT_FLAG
0,CASE WHEN
       INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG = 1
       AND
        TRANS_ORDER = 1
       THEN
        BASE.ACCRD_INT
       ELSE
        0
       END AS
        FORECAST_INTEREST_CHARGE
 ,LAG(POSTED_DTE) OVER (PARTITION BY SUBT1.UNIQUE_KEY_INT
 ORDER BY
  SUBT1.POSTED_DTE) AS LAGGED_POSTED_DTE
 ,CASE WHEN SUBT1.POSTED_DTE < SUBT1.MIN_REPAYMENT
      THEN
       0
      ELSE
       1
 END AS
  FIRST_RPMT_FLAG

  SQRSBL30_PRD_RSDL_BAL_CAL SUBT1

 OR
  (SUBT1.INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG = 1 AND FIRST_RPMT_FLAG = 0)
 OR
  (SUBT1.INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG = 1 AND FIRST_RPMT_FLAG = 0)
 OR
  (SUBT1.INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG = 1

 AND
  SUBT1.PAYMENT_COUNTER IS NOT NULL)

       THEN
        1
       WHEN
        NAPS_REPAYMT_NO = PAYMENT_COUNTER
       AND
        PAYMENT_FLAG <> 1
      THEN
       2
      ELSE
       2
 END AS
  CLOSED_IND

 INSERT INTO SQRSBL30_RESI_BAL_RCRSN
 WITH RECURSIVE REC_TABLE
 (
   WH_ACC_NO
  ,UNIQUE_KEY_INT
  ,EXPIRY_DTE
  ,EXPIRY_NXT_WORKDAY_DTE
  ,POSTED_DTE
  ,DATE_ROW_NUMBER
  ,NAPS_REPAYMT_NO
  ,INT_RTE
  ,INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG
  ,INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG
  ,NO_DAYS
  ,PAYMENT_COUNTER
  ,PAYMENT_FLAG
  ,REPAYMT_AMT_FINAL
  ,FORECAST_INTEREST_DAILY
  ,FORECAST_INTEREST_CUMULATIVE
  ,FORECAST_INTEREST_CHARGE
  ,BALANCE
  ,FORECAST_TOTAL_INTEREST
  ,FORECAST_TOTAL_REPAYMENTS

  ,PAYMENT_BALANCE

  ,BAL_NO_PAYMENT_CUMULATIVE
  ,BAL_NO_PAYMENT_CHARGE
  ,BAL_NO_PAYMENT_BALANCE
0)   AS
0(SELECT
  WH_ACC_NO
 ,UNIQUE_KEY_INT
 ,RBS.EXPIRY_DTE
 ,RBS.EXPIRY_NXT_WORKDAY_DTE
 ,CAST(POSTED_DTE AS DATE FORMAT 'YYYY-MM-DD')
 ,DATE_ROW_NUMBER
 ,NAPS_REPAYMT_NO
 ,INT_RTE
 ,INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG
 ,INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG
 ,NO_DAYS
 ,RBS.PAYMENT_COUNTER
 ,RBS.PAYMENT_FLAG
 ,RBS.REPAYMT_AMT_FINAL
 ,CAST(RBS.FORECAST_INTEREST_DAILY      AS DECIMAL (15,5))
 , CASE WHEN INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG = 1
           THEN 0.00
           ELSE
            CAST(RBS.ACCRUED_INTEREST_FORECAST*-1 AS DECIMAL(15,5))
           END AS
            FORECAST_INTEREST_CUMULATIVE
 ,CAST(RBS.FORECAST_INTEREST_CHARGE     AS DECIMAL (15,2))
                   AS FORECAST_INTEREST_CHARGE
 ,CAST(RBS.BALANCE AS DECIMAL(15,2))
 ,CASE WHEN
        INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG = 1
       THEN
        0.00
      ELSE
       CAST(RBS.ACCRUED_INTEREST_FORECAST*-1
       AS DECIMAL(15,2))
 END AS
  FORECAST_TOTAL_INTEREST
 ,RBS.FORECAST_TOTAL_REPAYMENTS
 ,CAST(0.00000 AS DECIMAL (15,6)) AS PAYMENT_INTEREST_DAILY
 ,CAST(0.00000 AS DECIMAL (15,6)) AS PAYMENT_INTEREST_CUMULATIVE
 ,CAST(0.00000 AS DECIMAL (15,6)) AS PAYMENT_INTEREST_CHARGE
 ,CAST(COALESCE (RBS.PAYMENT_COUNTER, 0) AS DECIMAL (15,6))
  AS PAYMENT_BALANCE
 ,CAST(RBS.FORECAST_INTEREST_DAILY AS DECIMAL (15,6))
 ,CASE WHEN
    INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG = 1
      THEN CAST(0.00000
      AS    DECIMAL (15,2))
 ELSE
  CAST(RBS.ACCRUED_INTEREST_FORECAST*-1 AS DECIMAL(15,2))
 END
 ,CAST(RBS.FORECAST_INTEREST_CHARGE AS DECIMAL (15,2))
 ,CAST(RBS.BALANCE AS DECIMAL(15,2))

   SQRSBL30_PRD_RESI_BAL_FLAGS RBS

   RBS.DATE_ROW_NUMBER = 1

0SELECT
  RBS.WH_ACC_NO
 ,RBS.UNIQUE_KEY_INT
 ,RBS.EXPIRY_DTE
 ,RBS.EXPIRY_NXT_WORKDAY_DTE
 ,RBS.POSTED_DTE
 ,RBS.DATE_ROW_NUMBER

 ,RBS.REPAYMT_AMT_FINAL
 ,RT.BALANCE * RBS.INT_RTE/100 * RBS.NO_DAYS /365
0,CASE WHEN
        RT.INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG =1
       THEN
        RT.BALANCE * RBS.INT_RTE/100 * RBS.NO_DAYS /365
       ELSE
        RT.FORECAST_INTEREST_CUMULATIVE + RT.BALANCE *
        RBS.INT_RTE/100 * RBS.NO_DAYS /365
 END AS
    INT_CUM
0,CASE WHEN
        RT.INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG =1
       THEN
        TRUNC(RT.BALANCE * RBS.INT_RTE/100 * RBS.NO_DAYS /365 , 2)
       WHEN
        RBS.PAYMENT_COUNTER    = RBS.NAPS_REPAYMT_NO
       AND
        RBS.PAYMENT_COUNTER - RT.PAYMENT_COUNTER = 1
       THEN
        TRUNC(RT.FORECAST_INTEREST_CUMULATIVE +
        RT.BALANCE * RBS.INT_RTE/100 * RBS.NO_DAYS /365 , 2)
       WHEN
        RBS.INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG = 1
       THEN
        TRUNC(RT.FORECAST_INTEREST_CUMULATIVE + RT.BALANCE *
        RBS.INT_RTE/100 * RBS.NO_DAYS /365, 2)
       ELSE
        0
 END AS
  INT_CH
0,CASE WHEN
        RBS.INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG = 1
        AND COALESCE(RBS.PAYMENT_COUNTER,0) -
        COALESCE(RT.PAYMENT_COUNTER,0) >= 1
       THEN
        RT.BALANCE + INT_CH + RBS.REPAYMT_AMT_FINAL
       WHEN
        RBS.INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG = 0
       AND
        COALESCE(RBS.PAYMENT_COUNTER,0) -
        COALESCE(RT.PAYMENT_COUNTER,0) <= 0
       THEN
        RT.BALANCE
       WHEN
        RBS.INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG = 1
       AND
        COALESCE(RBS.PAYMENT_COUNTER,0) -
        COALESCE(RT.PAYMENT_COUNTER,0) <= 0
       THEN
        RT.BALANCE + INT_CH
       WHEN
        RBS.INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG = 0
       AND
        COALESCE(RBS.PAYMENT_COUNTER,0) -
       COALESCE(RT.PAYMENT_COUNTER,0) >= 1
      THEN
       RT.BALANCE + RBS.REPAYMT_AMT_FINAL
 END
 ,RT.FORECAST_TOTAL_INTEREST + RT.BALANCE * RBS.INT_RTE/100 *
  RBS.NO_DAYS /365
 ,CASE WHEN
    RBS.PAYMENT_COUNTER - RT.PAYMENT_COUNTER = 1
      THEN
       RT.FORECAST_TOTAL_REPAYMENTS + RBS.REPAYMT_AMT_FINAL
      ELSE
       RT.FORECAST_TOTAL_REPAYMENTS
 END
 ,CAST(RT.PAYMENT_BALANCE * RBS.INT_RTE/100 *
 RBS.NO_DAYS /365 AS DECIMAL (15,6))
 ,CAST(CASE WHEN RT.INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG =1
            THEN
             RT.PAYMENT_BALANCE * RBS.INT_RTE/100 * RBS.NO_DAYS /365
            ELSE
             RT.PAYMENT_INTEREST_CUMULATIVE + RT.PAYMENT_BALANCE *
             RBS.INT_RTE/100 * RBS.NO_DAYS /365
 END AS
  DECIMAL (15,6))
0,CAST(CASE WHEN COALESCE(RBS.PAYMENT_COUNTER, RT.PAYMENT_COUNTER)
 = RBS.NAPS_REPAYMT_NO AND COALESCE(RBS.PAYMENT_COUNTER,
   RT.PAYMENT_COUNTER) - RT.PAYMENT_COUNTER = 1
      THEN
       RT.PAYMENT_INTEREST_CUMULATIVE + RT.PAYMENT_BALANCE *
       RBS.INT_RTE/100 * RBS.NO_DAYS /365
      WHEN
       RBS.INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG = 1
      THEN
       RT.PAYMENT_INTEREST_CUMULATIVE + RT.PAYMENT_BALANCE
       * RBS.INT_RTE/100 * RBS.NO_DAYS /365
      ELSE
       0
 END
 AS DECIMAL (15,6)) AS PAYMENT_INT_CH
0,CAST(CASE WHEN RBS.INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG = 1
       THEN
        RT.PAYMENT_BALANCE + PAYMENT_INT_CH +
        COALESCE(RBS.PAYMENT_COUNTER, RT.PAYMENT_COUNTER) -
        COALESCE(RT.PAYMENT_COUNTER, 1)
       ELSE
        RT.PAYMENT_BALANCE +
        COALESCE(RBS.PAYMENT_COUNTER, RT.PAYMENT_COUNTER) -
        COALESCE(RT.PAYMENT_COUNTER, 1)
 END AS DECIMAL (15,6))
0,CAST(RT.BAL_NO_PAYMENT_BALANCE * RBS.INT_RTE/100 * RBS.NO_DAYS /365
 AS DECIMAL (15,6))
 ,CAST(CASE WHEN RT.INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG =1
        THEN
         RT.BAL_NO_PAYMENT_BALANCE * RBS.INT_RTE/100 *
         RBS.NO_DAYS /365
        ELSE
         RT.BAL_NO_PAYMENT_CUMULATIVE +
         RT.BAL_NO_PAYMENT_BALANCE * RBS.INT_RTE/100 *
         RBS.NO_DAYS /365
 END AS DECIMAL (15,2))
0,CAST(CASE WHEN COALESCE(RBS.PAYMENT_COUNTER, RT.PAYMENT_COUNTER)
 = RBS.NAPS_REPAYMT_NO AND COALESCE(RBS.PAYMENT_COUNTER,
 RT.PAYMENT_COUNTER) - RT.PAYMENT_COUNTER = 1
      THEN
       RT.BAL_NO_PAYMENT_CUMULATIVE +
       RT.BAL_NO_PAYMENT_BALANCE * RBS.INT_RTE/100 *
       RBS.NO_DAYS /365
      WHEN
       RBS.INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG = 1
      THEN
       RT.BAL_NO_PAYMENT_CUMULATIVE +
       RT.BAL_NO_PAYMENT_BALANCE * RBS.INT_RTE/100 *
       RBS.NO_DAYS /365
      ELSE
       0
 END AS
  DECIMAL (15,2))
 AS
  BAL_NO_PAYMENT_INT_CH
0,CAST(CASE WHEN RBS.INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG = 1
        THEN
         RT.BAL_NO_PAYMENT_BALANCE + BAL_NO_PAYMENT_INT_CH
        ELSE
         RT.BAL_NO_PAYMENT_BALANCE
 END AS DECIMAL (15,2))
0FROM

0INNER JOIN

 ON

 /* POPULATE RSDL_BAL_CALC_PERIODIC                                   */
 /*********************************************************************/
 INSERT INTO RSDL_BAL_CALC_PERIODIC
 (
  WH_ACC_NO
 ,ACC_NO
 ,APPL_ID
 ,APPL_PROD_ID
 ,UNIQUE_KEY_INT
 ,MIN_REPAYMENT
 ,TERM_NO_UNITS
 ,REPAYMT_AMT
 ,REPAYMT_UNIT
 ,REPAYMT_INTERVAL
 ,REPAYMT_DAY
 ,ACCRD_INT
 ,CURRENT_CNTRACT_MATURITY_DTE
 ,CNTRACT_MATURITY_DTE
 ,NXT_REPRICING_DTE
 ,PROD_CDE
 ,REPAYMT_EXCPTN_DESCR
 ,EXPIRY_DTE
 ,EXPIRY_NXT_WORKDAY_DTE
 ,POSTED_DTE
 ,DATE_ROW_NUMBER
 ,NAPS_REPAYMT_NO
 ,INT_RTE
 ,INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG
 ,INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG
 ,NO_DAYS
 ,PAYMENT_COUNTER
 ,PAYMENT_FLAG
 ,REPAYMT_AMT_FINAL
 ,DR_INT_CAT
 ,NET_BAL_FOR_PRNCPL
 ,RESIDUAL_BAL_AMT
 ,RESIDUAL_BAL_IND
 ,EXCLUDED_IND
 ,DIFF_EXPIRY_DATES
 ,WEEKLY_REPAYMTS
 ,REPAYMT_AMT_0
 ,REPAYMT_INTERVAL_0
 ,OTHER_REPAYMT_TYPE
 ,NON_NAPS
 ,EXPIRED
 ,REASON
 ,PERIOD_DTE
 ,SRCE_SYS
 ,SRCE_INST
 ,LOAD_DTE
 ,LOAD_TIME
 )
0SELECT
   BASE.WH_ACC_NO
  ,BASE.ACC_NO
  ,BASE.APPL_ID
  ,BASE.APPL_PROD_ID
  ,BASE.UNIQUE_KEY_INT
  ,CASE WHEN EXCLUDED_IND = 'Y'
        THEN
         NULL
        ELSE
         BASE.MIN_REPAYMENT
   END AS MIN_REPAYMENT
0 ,BASE.TERM_NO_UNITS
  ,BASE.REPAYMT_AMT
  ,BASE.REPAYMT_UNIT
  ,BASE.REPAYMT_INTERVAL
  ,BASE.REPAYMT_DAY
  ,BASE.ACCRD_INT
  ,BASE.CURRENT_CNTRACT_MATURITY_DTE
  ,BASE.CNTRACT_MATURITY_DTE
  ,BASE.NXT_REPRICING_DTE
  ,BASE.PROD_CDE
  ,BASE.REPAYMT_EXCPTN_DESCR
  ,BASE.EXPIRY_DTE
  ,BASE.EXPIRY_NXT_WORKDAY_DTE
  ,RB.POSTED_DTE
  ,RB.DATE_ROW_NUMBER
  ,BASE.NAPS_REPAYMT_NO
  ,BASE.INT_RTE
  ,RB.INTEREST_ACCRUAL_FLAG
  ,RB.INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG
  ,RB.NO_DAYS
  ,RB.PAYMENT_COUNTER
  ,RB.PAYMENT_FLAG
  ,BASE.REPAYMT_AMT AS REPAYMT_AMT_FINAL
  ,BASE.DR_INT_CAT
  ,BASE.NET_BAL_FOR_PRNCPL
  ,CASE WHEN
    RB.INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG = 1
   AND
   RB.PAYMENT_FLAG = 1
   AND
   RB.BALANCE < 0
        THEN
         CAST(RB.BALANCE AS DECIMAL(18,2))
        WHEN
         RB.INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG = 1
        AND
         RB.PAYMENT_FLAG = 1
        AND
         RB.BALANCE >= 0
        THEN
        CAST(0 AS DECIMAL(18,2))
       WHEN
        RB.INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG = 1
       AND
        RB.POSTED_DTE = RB.EXPIRY_NXT_WORKDAY_DTE
       AND
        RB.BALANCE < 0
       THEN
        CAST(RB.BALANCE AS DECIMAL(18,2))
       WHEN
        RB.INTEREST_POSTED_FLAG = 1
       AND
        RB.POSTED_DTE = RB.EXPIRY_NXT_WORKDAY_DTE
       AND
        RB.BALANCE >= 0
       THEN
        CAST(0 AS DECIMAL(18,2))
       WHEN
        RB.BALANCE + TRUNC(RB.FORECAST_INTEREST_CUMULATIVE) < 0
       THEN
        CAST(RB.BALANCE + TRUNC(RB.FORECAST_INTEREST_CUMULATIVE,2)
        AS DECIMAL(18,2))
       WHEN
        RB.BALANCE + TRUNC(RB.FORECAST_INTEREST_CUMULATIVE) > 0
       THEN
        0
       ELSE
        0
 END AS
  RESIDUAL_BAL_AMT
0,CASE WHEN RESIDUAL_BAL_AMT < 0
       THEN
        1
       ELSE
        0
 END AS
  RESIDUAL_BAL_IND
 ,BASE.EXCLUDED_IND
 ,DIFF_EXPIRY_DATES
 ,WEEKLY_REPAYMTS
 ,REPAYMT_AMT_0
 ,REPAYMT_INTERVAL_0
 ,OTHER_REPAYMT_TYPE
 ,NON_NAPS
 ,EXPIRED
 ,CASE WHEN DIFF_EXPIRY_DATES + WEEKLY_REPAYMTS +REPAYMT_AMT_0 +
  REPAYMT_INTERVAL_0 + OTHER_REPAYMT_TYPE + NON_NAPS + EXPIRED >1
       THEN
        'MULTIPLE'
       WHEN
      BASE.DIFF_EXPIRY_DATES = 1
     THEN
      COALESCE('EXPIRY DATES DIFFERENT','')
     WHEN
      BASE.WEEKLY_REPAYMTS = 1
     THEN
      COALESCE('WEEKLY REPAYMENTS','')
     WHEN
      BASE.REPAYMT_AMT_0 = 1
     THEN
      COALESCE ('REPAYMENT AMT IS 0', '')
     WHEN
      BASE.REPAYMT_INTERVAL_0 = 1
     THEN
      COALESCE ('REPAYMENT INTERVAL IS 0', '')
     WHEN
      BASE.OTHER_REPAYMT_TYPE = 1
     THEN
      COALESCE ('BULLET OR OTHER REPAYMT SCHED', '')
     WHEN
     BASE.NON_NAPS = 1
    THEN
     COALESCE ('NO NAPS APPLICATION', '')
    WHEN
     BASE.EXPIRED = 1
    THEN
     COALESCE ('EXPIRED ON REPORT DATE', '')
    WHEN
     EXCLUDED_IND = 'N'
    THEN
    'RESIDUAL BAL CALCD'
    ELSE
     NULL
 END AS
  REASON
 ,BASE.PERIOD_DTE
 ,BASE.APPL_SRCE_SYS AS SRCE_SYS
 ,BASE.SRCE_INST
 ,DATE AS LOAD_DTE
 ,TIME AS LOAD_TIME
0FROM
  SQRSBL10_RSDL_BAL_ACC_BASE BASE
0LEFT JOIN
  SQRSBL30_RESI_BAL_RCRSN RB
 ON
  RB.WH_ACC_NO = BASE.WH_ACC_NO
0WHERE
 (RB.POSTED_DTE <= RB.EXPIRY_NXT_WORKDAY_DTE
 OR
  RB.WH_ACC_NO IS NULL)
0QUALIFY
 ROW_NUMBER () OVER
 ( PARTITION BY BASE.WH_ACC_NO    ORDER BY
 RB.DATE_ROW_NUMBER DESC) =1;
0 *** Insert completed. 34213 rows added.
  *** Total elapsed time was 0.69 seconds.

0.IF ERRORCODE <> 0 THEN .QUIT 8
 +---------+---------+---------+---------+---------+---------+---------+---------+---------+---------+---------+---------+---------+-
0/**********************************************************************/
 /* COLLECT STATISTICS                                                 */
 /**********************************************************************/
 COLLECT STATISTICS
  INDEX (UNIQUE_KEY_INT, WH_ACC_NO)
 ,COLUMN (UNIQUE_KEY_INT)
 ,COLUMN (WH_ACC_NO)
 ,COLUMN (EXPIRY_DTE)
 ,COLUMN (EXPIRY_NXT_WORKDAY_DTE)
 ON RSDL_BAL_CALC_PERIODIC
 ;
0 *** Update completed. 6 rows changed.
  *** Total elapsed time was 0.80 seconds.

 .IF ERRORCODE <> 0 THEN .QUIT 8

  ,TOTAL_UPDATES
  ,TOTAL_DELETES

  ,LOAD_TIME

  )
0  SELECT
   'RSDL_BAL_CALC_PERIODIC'
  ,'SQREBL40'
  ,COALESCE(REB.SRCE_SYS, 0)
  ,REB.SRCE_INST
  ,COUNT(*)
  ,0
  ,0
  ,DTE.RUN_CONTROL_DTE
  ,DTE.LOAD_DTE
  ,DTE.LOAD_TIME
  ,DATE
  ,TIME
0  FROM

0  CROSS JOIN
    FDM_RUN_DATE DTE
0  GROUP BY
     DTE.RUN_CONTROL_DTE
    ,REB.SRCE_SYS

    ,DTE.LOAD_DTE
    ,DTE.LOAD_TIME
    ;
